# Agents · B · part 15

1,011 entries, builder to business-analyst. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **builder**: Primary implementer on Opus — carries out the substantive, judgement-requiring steps of an approved plan: non-trivial logic, wiring that needs decisions, anything where the "how" is not fully spelled out. Use for the parts a mechanical Sonnet implementer would get wrong. It writes code, but does NOT re-open the design. For purely mechanical steps (rote renames, applying a settled pattern) use the Sonnet `implementer` instead. Starts fresh with no conversation history — give it the plan step, the design intent, and the relevant file paths. ([marlls1989/claude-tiered-development](https://github.com/marlls1989/claude-tiered-development/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implements one fix or feature item from a written brief, on its own branch, with unit tests, evidence, a change note and a pull request. One-shot. Never merges, never edits an acceptance test. ([Martinhdeez/uve-harness](https://github.com/Martinhdeez/uve-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builder for the Proto plugin. Executes exactly one task file under docs/ux/.scratch/*/tasks/ and appends a Result section. Dispatched by the proto build and feedback skills; not for general coding. ([martylouis/agents](https://github.com/martylouis/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/proto/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implémentation substantielle ou indépendante dont la délégation amortit la coordination. Les modifications locales bornées restent à la racine. ([masskrdjn/claudeskills](https://github.com/masskrdjn/claudeskills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: You are the **builder** instance for SARApp. A separate Claude Code instance (the **tester**) writes tests, runs them, and reports findings against your code. You write production code and ship it through the handoff file to the tester. You do not write tests for your own code in the same session unless explicitly told to — the tester is your test author, and that separation is the point. ([mateuszmrowiec/SARApp](https://github.com/mateuszmrowiec/SARApp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implementation specialist. Executes subtasks using test-first development. Writes tests before implementation. ([MaxwellCalkin/Autobot](https://github.com/MaxwellCalkin/Autobot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md) · 2 copies)

- **buildline-try-run**: Reads a factory feature's spec, design and plan with no other context and says back what it would build, so gaps show up before any code is written. - **buildline-verifier**: Independently verifies a factory feature against its acceptance scenarios by running gates, the app, tests and browser checks, then reports PASS/FAIL with evidence. ([julianoczkowski/buildline](https://github.com/julianoczkowski/buildline/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/buildline-verifier.md) · ★13)
- **buildline-zoom-out**: Reads a factory feature's technical design with no other context and checks it for over-building, missed reuse, logic in the wrong place, and alternatives rejected without a concrete reason. ([julianoczkowski/buildline](https://github.com/julianoczkowski/buildline/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/buildline-zoom-out.md) · ★13)

- **bus_failures.py**: `LocalMessageBus.get_pending_messages`（`local_bus.py`）在一条消息的 `bus_message_failures.retry_count` 达到 3 时会把它永久过滤掉——这是防 poison-message 的机制，但代价是消息从此彻底消失，没有任何 UI 或 API 能看到它、 更谈不上恢复。`MessageBusTrigger._notify_permanent_failure` （`message_bus_trigger.py`）解决了"用户完全无感"的一半问题（往 inbox 写一条 通知），但通知之后用户仍然需要一个动作入口去清掉失败记录、让消息重新被投递 ——这个文件就是那个入口。是 NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus#52 修复的"可恢复"半边。 ([NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus](https://github.com/NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus/blob/HEAD/.mindflow/mirror/backend/routes/agents/bus_failures.py.md) · ★91)
